{"category":"insight","description":"S895 AC-AUTHORING RULE promoted from SA's core.hooksPath finding (#35399): the documented divergence check did NOT fail because it compared wrong -- it failed because it was pointed at a path it ASSUMED (.git/hooks) rather than RESOLVED (git config core.hooksPath -> .beads/hooks). Comparison logic fine; operand fiction. Generalized AC rule: ANY acceptance criterion of the form 'artifact A matches the artifact that RUNS' must specify how the running artifact is RESOLVED AT VERIFICATION TIME, never where it is ASSUMED to live; an AC naming a literal path for the executed artifact is defective on its face. Applies to CI gate ACs too (a gate AC must state which SHA/worktree it resolves and how, not 'builds the project'). Same defect class as negative-needs-positive-control: there an empty result was mistaken for a finding, here an IDENTICAL on the wrong pair was mistaken for a green -- the instrument answered honestly about something nobody wanted to know. RECURRENCE GAP raised to SA as friendly challenge: his structural fix (point core.hooksPath at eng/hooks, delete install-hooks.sh/verify-hooks-current.sh) closes the COPY but not the POINTER -- bd re-pointed core.hooksPath once unilaterally and nothing prevents the next bd upgrade/doctor/quickstart re-taking it, at which point divergence returns WITHOUT a check because the ruling deleted the verification scripts. Added AC-3 (drift): core.hooksPath verified to still resolve to tracked hook dir, verification hosted where bd cannot silently disable it -- explicitly NOT in pre-commit (a bd re-point routes around the very hook hosting the check); SessionStart CLI hook is immune by construction but must be COMMITTED (dev-team-6 mirror-image warning: live-not-committed is inert for everyone else). COMPOUNDING RISK flagged to PM: operator broadened --no-verify at 05:36Z; SA proved executed pre-commit already missing 5 committed guards at 05:43Z -- exposure is the PRODUCT of the two and nobody had both numbers in one place. Asked that any --no-verify use be logged with which guards were ALREADY inert so the morning brief states real coverage not assumed."}
{"category":"decision","description":"SELF-CORRECTION to my own S895 tier list (retraction reaching the ARTIFACT, not just the thread -- S890 lesson). In #35380 and in my earlier discovery-log entry I placed 'pinned action SHAs' in the tier I defined as 'a defect here lets a lie through the gate'. THAT PLACEMENT IS WRONG BY MY OWN CRITERION: an unpinned action cannot cause a gate to report a false PASS; it lets untrusted code execute -- a serious risk of a DIFFERENT class. My tier boundary was doing rhetorical work, not analytic work. The JUSTIFICATION survives (I labelled it 'supply-chain trust boundary', not 'Microsoft does this') but the TIER does not. Accepting PlatformDeveloper's demotion. CORRECTED SHAPE for SPEC: Tier1 = CAN REPORT A FALSE PASS only (hook-drift path resolution, committed-SHA-vs-worktree, gate-cannot-report-unearned-PASS). Supply-chain hardening (permissions:, action pinning) = separate lane, ranked below, justified on its own merits, NEVER justified as 'matching Microsoft'. Trigger: PlatformDeveloper #35406 self-corrected his own premise -- he was about to report 'Microsoft pins to SHAs, we don't', READ dotnet/runtime instead of recalling, found backport.yml references arcade backport-base.yml@main (a BRANCH pointer, weaker than a tag) => SHA-pinning is GitHub hardening guidance, NOT an observed Microsoft convention; labelled grep-confidence on 2 sampled workflows. Had that shipped I would have written an AC mandating 283 SHA pins on a nonexistent convention. ALSO: PlatformDeveloper found FOUR hook copies not three -- .git/hooks is BYTE-IDENTICAL to tracked, which is what makes the false all-clear convincing; no amount of rigour running that diff would ever catch it, confirming the AC must be 'resolve the executed path from git' not 'run the diff more carefully'. dev-team-6.md:40 PRINTS core.hooksPath and :41 IGNORES its output -- the rule-file correction is part of the deliverable, not a follow-up. AC SHARPENING sent to PM: his 'no future commit needs the bypass' success condition needs (1) a stated latency budget under adversarial conditions (AV live, bd daemon contending) proven across N consecutive runs, since 'no commit needs it' is only observable in hindsight; (2) pairing with a guard-coverage arm -- a gate that gets faster by checking LESS is a regression, and dropping guards is the cheapest way to hit a latency budget, which is exactly the state SA just found us in. Latency and coverage are ONE criterion with TWO arms."}
{"category":"decision","description":"AC-OWNER RULING (B2 retraction UPHELD, ProductManager): ProjectReviewer's withdrawal of the B2 clearance is CORRECT; DocumentationWriter's catch is right. Verified independently at committed HEAD before ruling: docs-site/docs/patterns/outbox.md L557-561 asserts unqualified 'monotonic fencing token so a superseded leader cannot claim or complete messages it no longer owns / closes the split-brain window', and L578-580 lists SQL Server among 'fencing-capable first-party stores' -- while sicpvm (OPEN P1) records the SqlServer guard is MAX(FencingToken) over MESSAGE ROWS which RESETS AFTER CLEANUP so a stale-token superseded leader passes. Page even documents outbox_fence control table for Postgres+Oracle (L477-485), making the SQL Server omission read as deliberate parity rather than a gap. THE FAULT IS THE AC'S, NOT THE IMPLEMENTER'S: sicpvm's own framing says 'Honesty caveats already in XML docs pending this' -- the criterion scoped the honesty surface to XML DOCS. Implementer hit it; reviewer swept src/** and correctly found it satisfied AGAINST THE CRITERION AS WRITTEN. Nobody failed the AC; the AC was defective, and I wrote it. CORRECTED CRITERION effective now: an honesty/caveat AC binds EVERY consumer-visible surface asserting the guarantee -- XML docs, docs-site/**, package README/release-notes, samples/** -- not whichever surface the criterion happened to name; a caveat reaching IntelliSense but not docs-site leaves the PRIMARY consumer channel asserting the false guarantee. Stays DOCUMENTED-PENDING not accepted (sicpvm remains OPEN P1 with the real durable-fence fix); no bd-/sprint/ADR IDs since docs-site is public surface. RECURRENCE FLAGGED: 2nd time a fix swept src/** and missed docs-site/** in the same file tree -- S890 34k958 shipped outbox DDL omitting error_message while the write path wrote it. f5-cross-project-test-sweep's S890 clause already covers 'the DDL we SHIP' but is scoped to SCHEMA/DDL; tonight is the same mechanism on a GUARANTEE CLAIM payload, so the clause didn't fire because nobody reads a caveat-correction as a schema change. This class is worse than B1/hook-divergence: those fail OUR gates, a stale consumer-facing doc fails on the CONSUMER's machine with no backstop behind it. Recommended to PM as WATCH not promotion (2 occurrences, gate wants >=3), natural home = clause-add generalizing DDL -> any shipped consumer-facing assertion, NOT a new file."}
{"category":"insight","description":"S895 FAILURE TAXONOMY -- THREE classes, not one (corrects my own #35380 single-tier collapse; 2nd time tonight my TIERING was the defective part, a pattern in my own work to watch). (1) GATE LIES: control runs, reports unearned PASS; fix = resolve the right operand (committed SHA, core.hooksPath). Instances: B1 built working tree not committed SHA; hook-drift gate verifies .git/hooks while git executes .beads/hooks. (2) GATE IS HOLLOW: control is REFERENCED but cannot run, absence is SILENT; fix = REFUSE on missing, never skip. Instance: PlatformDeveloper #35433 -- bd-comment-clobber-guard/bd-description-durability/bd-flush-guard scripts DELETED in 885dc509f ('retire obsolete daemon-era durability guards') but invocations left behind wrapped in 'if [ -f ]' -> silent no-op. Sharpest S890-thesis instance we have: the guard's own REFUSE branch reads 'a gate that cannot run is not a gate that found nothing' and is DEFEATED BY THE [ -f ] WRAPPER ENCLOSING IT -- a careful 3-state PASS/FAIL/REFUSE contract wrapped in an undeclared 4th state ABSENT->silence; REFUSE was built and [ -f ] reintroduced the exact failure REFUSE prevents, one level up where the contract cannot see it. ADOPTED AC verbatim: a guard reference to a missing script must REFUSE (loud, non-zero), never skip; '[ -f ] && run' is the anti-pattern because it makes deletion of a guard indistinguishable from a passing guard. (3) NO GATE EXISTS: shipped claim nothing ever verifies; fix = BUILD one. Instance: docs-site guarantee claims (ru8lwi). ALSO ACCEPTED ProjectReviewer #35432 challenge under rule-promotion-gate s6: my corrected honesty-AC in #35425 is PROSE with no exit 1 -- necessary as rationale, NOT a control, and I presented the ruling as if it closed the hole. It does not. His guarantee-claim parity check is the deliverable; my AC-rule is its rationale. Also accepted SHARED fault on B2 (his framing 'I let someone else's scope define my sweep' is the better generalisation; a criterion is a FLOOR not a ceiling -- my 'nobody failed the AC' was too clean and let review off the hook by construction). RAISED CONCRETE HOLE in the proposed parity gate: its trigger is guarantee VERBS (cannot/exactly-once/never/durable/monotonic). Checked vs the actual B2 text -- L557-561 contains 'cannot' so it is CAUGHT; L578-580 'Fencing-capable first-party stores are SQL Server, PostgreSQL, Oracle, MongoDB' contains NONE of those verbs so it is MISSED, and that half is arguably worse (a CAPABILITY ROSTER is what a consumer scans to pick a store). A verb-grep cannot see a claim asserted by MEMBERSHIP IN A LIST. Same under-enumeration class as the SHA-pinning premise: enumerate by the REAL indicator (every place the shipped surface names a store/subsystem in connection with a guarantee -- capability tables, support matrices, feature lists) not a convenient proxy. AC must state the PROPERTY not the MECHANISM per testing-patterns s3. Non-vacuity fixture: plant BOTH L557-561 AND L578-580, require RED on each; liveness = corrected page GREEN."}
{"category":"insight","description":"S895 BACKLOG SURVEY (ProductManager, delivering PM ask #34496). FOUR scoping-changing findings. (1) bd stats UNDER-REPORTS BLOCKED BY 25x: prints Blocked:3, bd list --status blocked --json returns 78. Arithmetic proves the list not the summary (373 open + 2 in_progress + 78 blocked + 9179 closed = 9632 = stated Total; with 3 it is 9557, 75 missing). REAL ACTIONABLE POOL = 453 not 373, and the 78 blocked contain THE ONLY TWO P0s IN THE BACKLOG, invisible to both bd stats and bd list --status open. (2) P0 4uubd1 PREMISE APPEARS STALE -- premise-gated vs mainline rather than escalated on the title (bead from 07-08, untouched since 07-11). Positive control FIRST (173 .cs files, greps returning hits, so a zero is a real zero): KeyDestructionOutcome IS adopted throughout tests/unit/Excalibur.Compliance.Tests; ZERO bool-typed assignments or ShouldBeTrue/False on DeleteKeyAsync; residual = 2 stale test METHOD NAMES (DeleteKeyAsync_ReturnsFalse/ReturnsTrue) encoding the bool era, misleading but NOT compile errors since 'var deleted = await' binds fine. CAVEAT STATED: did NOT compile, so this is 'the described breakage does not appear present', NOT 'the build is green'. Recommended re-grade P0->P3 naming cleanup after a build; did NOT close. A stale P0 sitting invisible makes the P0 count meaningless in both directions. (3) 6efifd -- likely S895 headline epic -- has ZERO CHILDREN: no bead links by parent/parent-child/any dependency, none mentions the string, no body beyond its title. ~28 beads are de-facto children (gate audit/deletion/repair), none formally linked => S895 cannot 'do the gate reform', it must DECOMPOSE IT FIRST (SPEC work, mine). Sub-theme = 22 measured gate-layer defects, several gates that pass while enforcing nothing (0iwn8h never runs; maaho4 never enforced; ejwkjh green on empty set; y322hc 7-of-9 harness locks unwired; ch6fkv 'section 6 says prose has no mechanism, and section 6 is prose'). That collection IS the evidence for 6efifd and argues its own thesis. ALSO vnbm5j priced P3 says 11/11 workflows RED on mainline -- if true, every 'move gating to CI' ambition migrates work onto a currently-failing platform => PRECONDITION not P3, verify before scoping the epic around CI. (4) PACKAGING GAP -- the CI/CD ask has a hole exactly where its most valuable work should be. Targeted grep across ALL 453 beads for SourceLink/PackageReleaseNotes/snupkg/symbol package/deterministic build/ContinuousIntegrationBuild/PackageValidation/PackageIcon/PackageLicense/CONTRIBUTING/CODE_OF_CONDUCT/semver returned EXACTLY ONE hit and only for an incidental ApiCompat mention. For a NuGet-deliverable project with a P1 '10.x release-readiness' epic there is NO TRACKED WORK for any of it. xnr76m: 93/93 samples reference source projects, 0 consume the .nupkg -- nothing here has ever exercised the install experience a consumer gets. The operator's bar is 'looks like something Microsoft would create or endorse' and the first thing a visitor encounters is the PACKAGE. This absence is a GAP IN THE BACKLOG not a cluster in it, and unlike the gate work it is invisible to every instrument we have, because you cannot have a false-PASS on a check that was never conceived. (5) P1 CARRIES NO SIGNAL: per 4z28da, 678 auto-created beads hardcoded P1 by log-discovery.sh, so any 'work the P1s' heuristic reads noise. CHEAP WINS: 5 confirmed duplicate pairs (p3xqab/v32po8, hvm9rt/l8q9ad, s5pgny/px02gj, 505y99/m7o4eq, hopk3z/s1esjj) -- SINGLE-ACTOR dedup only, the mutual-dedup race closes both; 10 blocked beads point only at CLOSED blockers (discovered-from is provenance not a blocker, one-line human check each); 63xsiv (Cosmos emulator non-functional) is the single highest-leverage unblock rooting ~8 blocked real-infra locks, needs operator env action = morning item; 4 tripwire/watch beads with no deliverable inflate P2/P3 counts (85dygz, 8yyuhk, f2fh2a, ocvwtp which self-describes as moot)."}
{"category":"decision","description":"STALE-GUIDANCE UPDATE (ProductManager correcting my own recommendation before it misleads, per retraction-reaches-the-artifact): in #35425 I told PM the shipped-consumer-artifact class was 'a WATCH not a promotion -- 2 occurrences, gate wants >=3'. THRESHOLD NOW MET, 3 instances: (1) S890 34k958 docs-site outbox DDL omitted error_message the write path writes; (2) S894 ru8lwi docs-site outbox guarantee claim asserts durable fencing SqlServer lacks; (3) tonight 6frgmy shipped event-store DDL, all 3 docs DDLs contradict the code AND each other. 6frgmy is the WORST: 1 and 2 were a wrong DETAIL in a doc, this is a CREATE TABLE that does not work at all in the subsystem that stores the consumer's data -- a consumer following it verbatim cannot persist an event. A recommendation that goes stale silently is the same defect class as a retraction that never reaches the artifact; nobody else was tracking the bar I set. GATE ALREADY EXISTS AND COVERS OUTBOX ONLY: eng/ci/shipped-ddl-sweep.sh MAP_ROWS has exactly TWO entries (postgres outbox via outboxTableName, sqlserver dbo.OutboxMessages via tableName). Event store, inbox, saga, projections ALL UNMAPPED -- which is exactly why 6frgmy shipped, the sweep was never looking at the event store. So the S895 item is NOT 'build a shipped-artifact gate' (told ProjectReviewer before he builds his #35432 parity gate) but 'the gate we have covers 2 tables of N -- extend the MAP to guarantee-critical subsystems and make the trigger enforce'. Cheaper than a new gate; DDL and guarantee-prose become two payload types on ONE shipped-surface-coverage sweep. PRIORITY INVERSION FLAGGED: ayundp (P3, assigned PlatformDeveloper, filed 07-18) verbatim names 'close the unmapped-but-write-pathed REFUSE-ships-uncaught hole (CI stricter than WARN)' -- the EXACT mechanism that let 6frgmy through -- while the defect it prevents just landed P0. Re-grade is PM's call. DIRECTION RISK FLAGGED: rsir7g (P2, filed as BUG) says 'shipped-ddl-sweep gate REFUSEs on pre-existing unmapped shipped-DDL, blocking docs-site commits' -- but the script's own header states the honesty property deliberately ('a shipped CREATE TABLE with no MAP entry is a REFUSE', not a pass). That is the S890 thesis implemented CORRECTLY and it is filed as a bug because it is inconvenient. Two opposite ways to close it: EXTEND THE MAP so unmapped becomes mapped and REFUSE stops firing because the gate can now verify (correct), or SUPPRESS/DOWNGRADE the REFUSE so the gate goes quiet while verifying nothing (manufactures the exact false-PASS we spent the night eliminating). STATED AC CONSTRAINT: the acceptance criterion for rsir7g is that unmapped shipped DDL becomes MAPPED, never that the REFUSE becomes SILENT; if friction cannot wait for full MAP coverage the fallback is a narrow explicitly-listed EXPIRING allowlist that still REFUSEs on anything not on it, never a blanket downgrade to WARN. Irony worth naming: the strongest gate we own is being experienced as the problem on the same night we proved five others were not running at all -- the quiet ones cost us months, the loud one cost us a docs commit."}
{"category":"insight","description":"SURFACE #5 CONFIRMED + docs/ RULE GAP + MY OWN 3RD ENUMERATION FAILURE (ProductManager, verified d8b5c6185, positive control passed). (1) docs/architecture/outbox-store-methods.md IFencedOutboxStore row asserts 'the caller passes its current fencing token so a superseded leader's writes are rejected... Both fenced operations are a single-statement atomic compare-and-swap... so there is NO CHECK-THEN-ACT WINDOW a demoted leader can interleave', Implemented by: SQL SERVER, PostgreSQL, Oracle. STRONGER claim than the docs-site one -- it asserts no window EXISTS, and for SqlServer (high-water resets on cleanup) that is precisely the window that does exist. 5th surface of the same false guarantee. (2) DocumentationWriter's rule-gap finding CONFIRMED with positive control (grep matches docs-site at :25, samples/ at :27): no-internal-refs-in-public-surface.md lists docs-site/, samples/, XML docs, README, CHANGELOG as applies-to and .claude/**, management/**, tests/**, .dts/**, commits, bd jsonl as does-NOT-apply -- docs/ IS IN NEITHER LIST. NOT THEORETICAL: that same file, same lines, contains '(Sprint 888, uw1nv4/f5zutu)' and '(Sprint 888, y1moc0)' -- sprint IDs and bead IDs committed in a public OSS repo. If docs/ is public surface that file is in violation RIGHT NOW, and the authors' own behaviour shows they read docs/ as internal. Routed to SoftwareArchitect for a taxonomy ruling with both sides stated fairly: PUBLIC (committed, world-visible on GitHub, and documentation-standards.md ALREADY rules ARCHITECTURE.md under src/** public because it ships in an OSS/NuGet framework -- docs/ is no less visible) vs INTERNAL (docs/architecture reads as engineering notes sibling to management/, and the sprint refs suggest authorial intent). My input: the distinction that matters to a consumer is not which folder but whether they can READ it, and on GitHub they can read all of it; lean public with management/** as the deliberate internal home. NOTE: if it goes internal the surface-#5 fix is STILL required -- a false guarantee is wrong in an internal doc too, just not a public-surface violation. (3) MY OWN #35425 RULING HAS THE IDENTICAL HOLE: I ruled the honesty AC binds 'XML docs, docs-site/**, package README/release notes, and samples/**' and DID NOT LIST docs/ -- so the criterion I wrote SPECIFICALLY to fix a too-narrow surface enumeration REPRODUCED THE EXACT DEFECT IT WAS CORRECTING, and surface #5 sits in the hole I left. THIRD enumeration failure tonight: #35380 put SHA-pinning in a tier my own criterion excluded (wrong tier); #35380 collapsed 3 failure classes into 1 (wrong taxonomy); #35425 enumerated public surfaces and missed one (wrong list). Same shape every time -- I produce a categorisation that is UNDER-INCLUSIVE. I flagged the pattern after #2 and did it again 30 min later, so self-awareness is not the fix. (4) STRUCTURAL FIX = the one I prescribed to ProjectReviewer (state the property, not the mechanism/list), per enforce-invariants-structurally: INVERT THE DEFAULT -- every committed artifact is PUBLIC surface unless it appears on the explicit internal-sink DENYLIST (management/**, .claude/**, .dts/**, tests/**, commit messages, tracker files, OPCOM); NO allowlist of public locations. Then docs/ needs no special-casing, 'we forgot to enumerate a surface' becomes INEXPRESSIBLE, a new directory is covered the day it is created rather than after it ships something false, and the gate becomes trivially writable (grep everything, subtract the denylist). Supersedes my #35425 enumeration. SA owns the rule-file shape; I stated the requirement."}
{"category":"decision","description":"S894 DEFINITION-OF-DONE MADE OPERATIONAL (ProductManager, AC owner, endorsing CEO's line as ruled by PM: 'we may ship a known GAP, we may NOT ship a false CLAIM'). THE SHARPENING THAT MAKES IT TERMINATE: read naively the line is fix-vs-defer, under which S894 never closes (5 surfaces + non-functional DDL + a 2nd fence bug is not a 6am fix list). But the test is NOT whether the DEFECT is fixed -- it is whether the SHIPPED ARTIFACT TELLS THE TRUTH ABOUT ITSELF. Every item therefore has TWO discharge paths: (1) fix the artifact so the claim becomes true, or (2) correct the claim so it matches reality -- the gap becomes STATED, and stating it is what makes it a gap rather than a lie. Terminating condition: S894 closes when NO SHIPPED ARTIFACT ASSERTS SOMETHING UNTRUE, not when every underlying defect is repaired. GUARD against option 2 becoming a rubber stamp (this is how a bar erodes): the correction must be AS PROMINENT AS THE CLAIM IT QUALIFIES, on the SAME surface at the SAME reading depth -- a confident heading with a caveat three paragraphs down is still a false claim in practice because the reader who acts on the heading never reaches the footnote; if the claim is in a capability table the correction goes IN THAT TABLE CELL, not in a note beneath it. TRIAGE (AC-owner call): BLOCKS CLOSE as FALSE CLAIMS = the 5 fencing surfaces (docs-site x2, XML docs, ARCHITECTURE.md, docs/architecture) + ru8lwi/q2m1t2 (deduped, same defect) + 6frgmy. CARRIES as GAPS = sicpvm (durable fence control table, OPEN P1), SA's 2nd fence bug (once filed, provided no surface claims it fixed), hook divergence + 3 dead guards (our tooling not shipped surface, S895 keystone), bd stats blocked under-report (internal instrument, S895). ON 6frgmy SPECIFICALLY: a CREATE TABLE is not neutral text, it is an INSTRUCTION ASSERTING 'run this and the product works'; TestsDeveloper measured EVERY column differing including the OUTPUT clause = total non-function; a consumer following it verbatim cannot persist an event and discovers it in THEIR environment with no gate of ours going red. It is a false claim and blocks -- BUT it has the same two discharge paths: if a correct canonical DDL cannot be authored AND VERIFIED at 6am, marking the three DDLs explicitly non-authoritative with a pointer to the code as source of truth is a LEGITIMATE close under this line. Ugly, honest, strictly better than shipping a schema that silently does not work. What is NOT available is leaving them standing as-is. DISTINCTION FROM THE OPERATOR'S 'no document-the-limitation deferrals' BAR (stated explicitly so it does not blur): PROHIBITED = 'feature does not work, note it and move on' with NO TRACKED WORK (accepting a limitation). PERMITTED HERE = the fix is tracked, prioritised and owned (sicpvm P1, SA's bug filed, 6frgmy filed) and the only thing landing tonight is making our published surface stop lying about it. We are not blessing the gap, we are refusing to misrepresent it while we still owe it -- the difference between a deferral and honest sequencing. NOTED: ProjectReviewer conceded the verb-trigger hole completely and corrected his parity gate to derive the trigger from the CODE side (pending-gap map) rather than prose -- structurally immune to a claim asserted by list-membership; he also named WHY he reached for verbs ('they were easy to grep') = the same convenient-proxy disease as my 3 enumeration failures, two of us landing on it independently in different artifacts within 2 hours suggests it is a property of the work not of either agent. He also verified shipped-ddl-sweep IS wired in the executed hook, closing my #35479 question -- the failure was COVERAGE (2 MAP rows, outbox only) not wiring. Sequencing and whether S894 closes tonight remain PM's call; I defined what 'done' ADMITS, not when to call it."}
{"category":"insight","description":"SHIPPED-DDL-SWEEP HAS A SECOND COVERAGE DIMENSION NOBODY NAMED + docs/ FORGOTTEN BY THREE INDEPENDENT ARTIFACTS (ProductManager, verified HEAD, positive control passed). ANSWERING HALF of DocumentationWriter's open question ('the gate is designed to REFUSE on unmapped DDL, so why did 6frgmy ship?'): eng/ci/shipped-ddl-sweep.sh:84 SHIPPED_DDL_DOC_ROOTS defaults to 'docs-site samples' -- docs/ IS NOT IN THE ROOTS. So coverage has TWO dimensions: MAP_ROWS (which tables: 2, outbox only -> event store would REFUSE IF SEEN) and DOC_ROOTS (which directories: docs-site+samples -> docs/ NEVER EXAMINED AT ALL). Event-store CREATE TABLEs exist in BOTH trees (control-passed grep): INSIDE roots = docs-site/docs/event-sourcing/event-store.md, docs-site/docs/configuration/event-store-setup.md, docs-site/docs/event-sourcing/migrations.md; OUTSIDE roots = docs/patterns/event-store-patterns.md, docs/advanced/security.md, docs/security/audit-logging.md. For the docs/ files the answer is SETTLED: the sweep never looked, it did not decline to refuse, it never reached the MAP. THE HALF THAT STAYS OPEN (not papering over it): the three docs-site/ files are INSIDE the roots and the gate SHOULD have REFUSEd and apparently did not -- unexplained, and the more important half. Two UNVERIFIED candidate leads offered as leads only: pre-commit path may be WARN-only (ayundp says the fix is 'CI stricter than WARN', implying WARN is today's behaviour); or the trigger may be scoped to STAGED files so a pre-existing false DDL nobody edits is never examined (which would also explain rsir7g's framing 'REFUSEs on PRE-EXISTING unmapped shipped-DDL' as the case where someone DOES touch one). Routed to PlatformDeveloper as the load-bearing question, with a proposed FOURTH failure mode: a gate that refuses correctly WHEN IT LOOKS but only looks at files someone happened to edit is not lying, not hollow, not absent -- it is SELECTIVELY BLIND. THE CONVERGENCE (strongest evidence of the night for invert-the-default): docs/ has now been forgotten by THREE INDEPENDENT ARTIFACTS by THREE DIFFERENT AUTHORS, each of whom enumerated carefully -- (1) no-internal-refs-in-public-surface.md applies-list names docs-site/ and samples/ but not docs/ (DocumentationWriter's finding); (2) shipped-ddl-sweep.sh DOC_ROOTS = docs-site samples, not docs/ (mine); (3) my own #35425 honesty-AC surface list (XML docs, docs-site/**, README, samples/**), not docs/ (my error). That is not three mistakes, it is ONE SYSTEMIC PROPERTY: docs/ sits in a shape where an allowlist author's eye slides past it every time, independently. Upgraded invert-the-default (#35494) from suggestion to recommendation to SA. SELF-REFINEMENT of my #35479: I wrote 'the failure was coverage (2 MAP rows, outbox only), not wiring' -- HALF RIGHT AND THE WRONG HALF IS LOAD-BEARING. The MAP gap is real but for docs/ files the sweep never REACHED the MAP; it was DOC_ROOTS, a dimension I did not know existed when I wrote that. My sentence would have sent someone to fix MAP_ROWS and they would have found docs/ still invisible afterward. Corrected: the failure is coverage in TWO independent dimensions (table-MAP and doc-roots) PLUS an unexplained non-REFUSE inside the roots; fixing only the MAP closes NONE of the docs/ surfaces. FOURTH correction tonight, all the same species -- an enumeration or categorisation of mine that was under-inclusive. Declared I will no longer hand-enumerate this sprint. On BackendDeveloper's catch that PM's terminating sweep was .md-only and missed 2 CODE surfaces (XML docs live in .cs): my #35505 triage DID say 'XML docs' so the list was right and the execution narrower, but I refused to fix it by publishing a better list -- given four demonstrated failures my hand-enumerated list is not trustworthy evidence of completeness. REQUIREMENT STATED AS A PROPERTY: the close sweep must be DERIVED MECHANICALLY from the claim across every committed path except the internal denylist, not from any human's inventory of where they think the claim lives; if the derivation cannot be run at this hour the honest close states 'corrected on the surfaces we enumerated; enumeration was manual and is not proven complete' -- a true statement about our confidence, materially different from asserting the sweep was exhaustive."}
{"category":"decision","description":"ARGUED AGAINST PROMOTING THE FIVE-ENUMERATION-FAILURES FINDING AS A RULE (ProductManager, owning 3-4 of the 5 myself, which is the standing to argue it). PM routed CEO's sprint-headline finding to RETRO as a rule-promotion candidate. Applied rule-promotion-gate s6 ('a rule is a TEACHING artifact, it does not fire -- ask where the GATE is'). THE FINDING REFUTES THE RULE IT IS ABOUT: the rule would say 'enumerate by the real indicator, watch for under-inclusive scopes' -- but all five failures were by people ALREADY DOING THAT. CEO ran grep -c and read the output and re-checked his own work; PM measured at the bytes; PM's .md-glob sweep USED A POSITIVE CONTROL; I verified every surface I listed; ProjectReviewer designed his verb-trigger deliberately. TWO of us had ALREADY INVOKED THIS EXACT PRINCIPLE AT OTHER PEOPLE within the preceding 2 hours (ProjectReviewer: 'they were easy to grep -- the precise error I flagged in others two hours ago, committed by me'; I flagged the pattern in myself after failure #2 then committed #3 and #4 anyway). A rule instructing carefulness is REFUTED BY ITS OWN DATASET -- if carefulness were the missing input, five careful people would not have produced five identical failures in one night. Promoting it would also be instance #11 of what ch6fkv already tracks ('s6 says prose has no mechanism, and s6 is prose'). WHAT ACTUALLY GENERALISES (narrower and more useful): AN ALLOWLIST OF SCOPES FAILS SILENTLY BY OMISSION; A DENYLIST FAILS LOUDLY BY INCLUSION. All five were allowlists -- a list of what to look at -- and in all five the defect was A MEMBER NOBODY THOUGHT OF; an allowlist is STRUCTURALLY INCAPABLE of announcing its own missing members because it has no way to know what is not on it. docs/ proves it: three independent artifacts, three authors, same omission, because docs/ occupies a shape an allowlist author's eye slides past every time independently. PROPOSED GATE (buildable, cheap, non-vacuous, catches all five): SCOPE-COMPLETENESS CHECK -- for each scope-defining artifact (shipped-ddl-sweep DOC_ROOTS, the public-surface rule applies-list, any sweep glob) assert covered_paths UNION internal_denylist == all_committed_paths; a committed path in NEITHER set FAILS, naming the unclassified path. SAFETY arm: add a new top-level directory, RED until classified -- today's tree is a ready-made RED fixture since docs/ is in neither set in all three artifacts, so the gate goes red on the current repo, which is exactly right. LIVENESS arm: a fully-classified repo PASSES so it cannot pass by refusing everything. Would have caught all three docs/ omissions ON THE DAY EACH ARTIFACT WAS WRITTEN, and makes 'forgot a surface' INEXPRESSIBLE rather than warned-against (enforce-invariants-structurally applied to scope definitions themselves). Composes with invert-the-default (#35494): once scopes are denylist-shaped this gate is HOW you enforce the denylist stays complete -- one mechanism, two problems. RECOMMENDED TO RETRO: YES the gate as an S895 item; YES a clause on the existing scope-the-keystone-to-its-real-extent (which already owns 'enumerate by the REAL indicator not a proxy') adding the allowlist-vs-denylist distinction as its STRUCTURAL form, clause-add not new file, explicitly labelled as the gate's rationale; NO to a new rule file teaching careful enumeration -- refuted by its own evidence, taxes every session forever, and would be read by exactly the people who already knew it and failed anyway. NOTE TO CEO: the finding is STRONGER under this reading, not weaker -- 'five careful people failed identically' is a much sharper result than 'five people were careless' because it indicts the SHAPE OF THE ARTIFACT rather than the attention of the humans, and structural findings deserve structural fixes."}
{"category":"decision","description":"AC-OWNER RULING: THE DoD BINDS IN BOTH DIRECTIONS + THE META-CLAIM IS A CLAIM (ProductManager, landed before the correction sweep runs). (1) OVER-CORRECTION IS ALSO A FALSE CLAIM. ProjectReviewer #35544 measured 29 .cs files mentioning fencing+SqlServer and found MOST ARE TRUE STATEMENTS ABOUT A DIFFERENT ONE OF THIS REPO'S THREE DISTINCT FENCING MECHANISMS. A 'correction' that turns a true statement into a false one is shipping a false claim -- the DoD does not say 'remove text resembling the defect', it says do not ship falsehoods, and falsehood INTRODUCED by an over-eager sweep counts identically to falsehood left standing. DIRECTION IS IRRELEVANT TO THE LINE. RULED AC for the correction sweep: every edit must be verified against the SPECIFIC MECHANISM the statement refers to; a statement true of mechanism A must not be edited because it pattern-matches a false statement about mechanism B; PER-FILE VERIFICATION, NOT PER-PATTERN SUBSTITUTION; an unverified edit is not a correction, it is a NEW UNVERIFIED CLAIM and we would manufacture exactly what we spent the night removing. COROLLARY: a file that cannot be verified in the time available is LEFT UNTOUCHED and listed as unaudited -- untouched-and-disclosed is honest, edited-on-a-guess is not. (2) THE META-CLAIM IS A CLAIM -- the one that will bite at CLOSE. DocumentationWriter measured (no glob, positive control passed): 368 tracked files mention 'fenc', 88 on the PUBLIC surface, WE HAVE EXAMINED 5. So 'S894 shipped no false claims' WOULD ITSELF BE A FALSE CLAIM -- asserting the sweep was complete is an unearned PASS, the exact defect this sprint exists to eliminate, committed in the closing sentence of the sprint that eliminated it. Same structure as B1: an instrument (our own close statement) reporting a result it did not earn. REQUIRED CLOSE WORDING I can sign off on: 'N surfaces verified false and corrected. 88 public-surface files mention fencing; the remaining 83 are UNAUDITED. Enumeration was manual and is NOT proven complete. Mechanical derivation is tracked as S895 work.' That satisfies the DoD exactly -- ships a KNOWN GAP (unaudited surfaces, disclosed) and NO FALSE CLAIM (no asserted exhaustiveness we do not have). Deliberately an uncomfortable sentence to write at 6:50am; the discomfort is the sentence doing its job. (3) SHARPENED DocumentationWriter's point that hand-enumeration provably cannot terminate: 88 is not a big number, it is an UNBOUNDED one, because nobody can prove 88 is the whole population either -- it is git grep 'fenc', which misses any surface asserting the guarantee without that substring (e.g. a capability table listing SqlServer under a 'durable' column, exactly the L578-580 shape). THE DENOMINATOR IS ITSELF AN ALLOWLIST -- 4th artifact tonight whose scope was defined by what someone thought to look for. Refused to propose a better search string (that would be my 5th enumeration failure); the requirement is MECHANICAL DERIVATION from the claim and the gate is the scope-completeness check in #35541. (4) PlatformDeveloper retracted his own #35502: the event-store DDL is not unmapped/REFUSE, it is INVISIBLE -- the gate's regex cannot see bracketed [schema].[Table], so NO PASS, NO REFUSE, NO SIGNAL. That is a FIFTH state below the three-state contract entirely: not-seen. REFUSE at least announces 'I could not verify this'; INVISIBLE announces nothing, and an invisible surface is INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM A CLEAN ONE in every log we keep. This retires my own #35526 explanation as INCOMPLETE rather than wrong: DOC_ROOTS explains the three docs/ files, the regex blindness explains the docs-site/ ones that were inside the roots and still produced no signal -- the open half I flagged, closed by him, and the answer is worse than either of us guessed. Credit: he ran the gate instead of inferring from MAP_ROWS, which is exactly the inference I also made."}
{"category":"decision","description":"STOP ENDORSED, STANDING DOWN (ProductManager, final analytical position on S894). (1) ACCEPTED TestsDeveloper's correction of my theory: 6frgmy lives at docs-site/docs/configuration/event-store-setup.md:280 and SHIPPED_DDL_DOC_ROOTS defaults to 'docs-site samples', so the file IS in scope -- the gate SAW it, REFUSED, and REFUSE IS DELIBERATELY WARN-ONLY. My docs/-exclusion is NOT the cause of 6frgmy. It stands as a real SEPARATE second gap (the three docs/ files genuinely are never examined) but does not explain the defect I invoked it for, and I would rather it be corrected than let a plausible-but-wrong mechanism harden into S895 scope. WHAT HELD: I labelled the docs-site half GENUINELY OPEN and offered WARN-only as an UNVERIFIED LEAD rather than an answer; TestsDeveloper ran it and closed it. That is the difference between the hedge working and getting lucky, worth naming on a night when four of my enumerations did not hold. ROOT CAUSE OF RECORD: unmapped => REFUSE => WARN-only => coverage debt is silently shippable -- cleaner and more actionable S895 target than either of my explanations. (2) MY ACCEPTANCE POSITION IS FINAL; PM IS NOT BLOCKED ON ME. Delivered and needing no further input: DoD operational (ship a known gap, never a false claim; discharge by fixing the artifact OR correcting the claim; correction as prominent as the claim it qualifies); binds BOTH directions (an over-correction making a true statement false is shipping a false claim; per-file mechanism verification; unverifiable files left untouched and disclosed -- now moot given PM's sweep stop, which is the correct resolution); and the CLOSE WORDING I can sign off on: 'N surfaces verified false and corrected. 88 public-surface files mention fencing; the remainder are UNAUDITED. Enumeration was manual and is NOT proven complete. Mechanical derivation tracked as S895 work.' If the close says that, S894 closes clean by my criteria. (3) WHY I AM STOPPING RATHER THAN CONTINUING TO BE USEFUL -- retro material: the regress CEO terminated had a specific shape, EVERY NEW MEASUREMENT WAS CORRECT AND EACH ONE CREATED TWO MORE. I contributed to it (the docs/ finding, the DOC_ROOTS dimension, the denominator sharpening) -- all true, ALL OF IT ALSO FUEL. A sprint that cannot stop finding things has stopped being able to ship them. The 83 unaudited surfaces are REAL and DISCLOSED, and disclosed-and-tracked is a legitimate close under the line we ruled; continuing to enumerate them tonight would produce more true findings and ZERO additional shipped value, and would quietly convert an honest gap into an unbounded one. Not in the critical path for DOCS/CLOSE/RETRO. Standing by awake and deliberately quiet."}
{"category":"decision","description":"UPGRADED MY OWN CLOSE WORDING (ProductManager, AC owner \u2014 correcting my gate because its INPUT changed, not new investigation). In #35526 I required the close sweep be DERIVED MECHANICALLY FROM THE CLAIM, not from a human's inventory. BackendDeveloper delivered exactly that: SEARCH THE CLAIM SENTENCE, NOT THE TOPIC KEYWORD -> 368 topic-mentions collapse to 5 claim-sites. Independently cross-checked: ProjectManager ran his own broader pattern (37 hits, mostly TRUE statements about inbox/saga claim-atomicity), compared, ADOPTED BD's list over his own, and the check found ZERO NEW false claims. That is mechanical derivation plus an independent converging cross-check -- NOT a manual inventory -- so my prior caveat ('88 public-surface files mention fencing; the remainder are UNAUDITED; enumeration was MANUAL and NOT PROVEN COMPLETE') is now FALSE IN BOTH CLAUSES and I must not force it. KEY PRINCIPLE STATED: UNDERSTATING CONFIDENCE IS NOT THE SAFE DIRECTION, IT IS THE SAME DEFECT POINTED THE OTHER WAY. I spent the night arguing the meta-claim is a claim; 'we don't really know' is ALSO a claim, and if the evidence no longer supports it, asserting it ships a falsehood about our own rigour. Clinging to a pessimistic sentence because pessimism feels safer at 7am would be failing my own rule in order to LOOK cautious. REVISED CLOSE WORDING I can sign off on (supersedes #35571): '5 false-claim sites identified by mechanical derivation from the claim sentence (368 topic-mentions -> 5 claim-sites), independently cross-checked by a second agent using a broader pattern which found ZERO additional false claims. The 88 topic-mentions were NOT individually audited -- the discriminator SUPERSEDES that count rather than leaving it outstanding. This is convergent mechanical evidence, NOT a proof of exhaustiveness: the derivation is a pattern over claim phrasings, so a claim asserted in a form the pattern does not match (e.g. by table membership) would not appear. Generalising the discriminator into a standing gate is tracked as S895 work.' Materially stronger than the original and still honest about the one real residual \u2014 and the residual is now SPECIFIC AND BOUNDED ('a claim shaped unlike the pattern') rather than the vague unbounded '83 unaudited files'. A NAMED LIMITATION IS A GAP; AN UNBOUNDED ONE IS JUST ANXIETY WITH A NUMBER ATTACHED. RETRO MATERIAL: BackendDeveloper's discriminator is the most valuable single artifact of the night, above any bead \u2014 five of us INCLUDING ME kept widening the SEARCH SPACE (.md -> .cs -> all-extensions -> 368 files) when the actual fix was to NARROW THE SEARCH TARGET from the topic to the assertion; every one of us was tuning the wrong dimension. Counterpart to the enumeration-failure finding: THE FIVE FAILURES WERE ALL ALLOWLISTS OF PLACES; THE FIX WAS A DERIVATION FROM THE CLAIM. Also noted: ProjectManager disclosed breaking his own no-more-sweeps rule to run the comparison, and the comparison showed HIS list was worse \u2014 doing that and publishing it that way round is why the team can trust the adopted list."}
{"category":"insight","description":"CROSSED-MESSAGE COLLISION FLAGGED (ProductManager, flag only \u2014 ran nothing, reopened nothing). At 06:58Z ProjectManager published '6frgmy ROOT CAUSE ADJUDICATED AT THE BYTES \u2014 TestsDeveloper is RIGHT' (warn-only REFUSE branch at .beads/hooks/pre-commit:158-163, the *) case prints a warning and does not exit). At 06:58Z \u2014 THE SAME MINUTE \u2014 TestsDeveloper published 'RECONCILED: PlatformDeveloper is RIGHT, I was WRONG' (the gate is BLIND to bracketed [Schema].[Table]; event-store-setup.md appears NOWHERE in a COMPLETED sweep, GATE_EXIT=2 ELAPSED_SEC=546, exit captured directly with no pipe; a MAP row would NOT have helped). NEITHER SAW THE OTHER. PM's adjudication therefore rests on a claim ITS OWN AUTHOR WITHDREW AS HE WAS WRITING IT \u2014 the same crossed-message shape that produced the 4-flip guard saga two hours earlier (CEO vs TestsDeveloper, messages 11 seconds apart). RESOLUTION OFFERED WITHOUT ANY NEW MEASUREMENT: THE TWO MECHANISMS ARE NOT MUTUALLY EXCLUSIVE AND BOTH CAN BE TRUE \u2014 they are LAYERED, NOT COMPETING. BLINDNESS explains why 6frgmy SPECIFICALLY produced no signal (the regex cannot see [Schema].[Table] so the file never appears in the sweep at all: no PASS, no REFUSE, no signal). WARN-ONLY explains why the 13 tables that WERE reported (events, dbo.DeadLetter..., etc.) shipped regardless (the *) branch prints 'REFUSE ... NOT BLOCKING' and does not exit). The file never reached the branch; the branch would not have stopped it if it had. If that reading holds the honest root cause of record is BOTH, IN SEQUENCE \u2014 and a MAP row genuinely would not have helped, which is PlatformDeveloper's point and the one with S895 consequences (fixing MAP_ROWS closes nothing for bracketed-name DDL). ASKED PM to confirm which mechanism the close/brief states, or state both \u2014 a one-line reconciliation between two agents who already hold the evidence. EXPLICITLY DID NOT ask anyone to measure: DocumentationWriter was right to flag-not-measure under CEO's stop, TestsDeveloper already ran the expensive 546s check with the exit captured directly per the no-pipe-masked-exit rule, and PlatformDeveloper ran the gate rather than inferring from config. THE EVIDENCE EXISTS; ONLY THE RECONCILIATION IS MISSING. WHY I BROKE SILENCE UNDER THE STOP: a root cause that is WRONG IN THE OPERATOR BRIEF is a false claim in the artifact the operator reads FIRST \u2014 the exact category the DoD prohibits, in the document reporting our compliance with it. Cheap to prevent now, embarrassing to discover at breakfast."}
{"category":"decision","description":"AC RULING: DocumentationWriter's REFUSE is CORRECT and does NOT block close (ProductManager). He finished his doc edits and REFUSED to report them validated because the docs-site build gate could not execute (REAL BUILD_EXIT=1, 'Cannot find module ../scope/index.js', node_modules/@babel/traverse/lib/scope/ EMPTY on disk since 2026-07-20 = corrupt npm install, not a content defect). That is the sprint's entire thesis applied by the person it cost the most, at 7am, when reporting PASS would have closed his lane. RULING \u2014 THESE ARE TWO SEPARATE CLAIMS AND CONFLATING THEM IS THE ERROR: (a) 'the corrected text is accurate' IS verifiable tonight by MECHANISM VERIFICATION (what my AC required, and what SoftwareArchitect's exact replacement text supplies) => ACCEPT; (b) 'docs-site still builds' is NOT verifiable, the gate cannot execute => DISCLOSE AS UNVALIDATED. A dead build gate does not invalidate a mechanism-verified content correction, it only means we cannot ALSO claim the site compiles. Rolling back honest content fixes because an unrelated toolchain is broken would trade a real improvement for a false comfort. THE CLOSE MUST NOT SAY 'docs validated' or 'docs-site green' \u2014 per my meta-claim ruling, asserting a validation we did not obtain is precisely the unearned PASS this sprint exists to eliminate, committed in the closing sentence again. REQUIRED WORDING: 'Consumer-doc corrections were verified for content against the implementation. The mitigation we were about to ship to consumers DID NOT WORK \u2014 retention cannot defend against either mechanism. {"category":"decision","description":"PRESERVATION OUTRANKS CLOSE \u2014 I RELEASED ALL CLOSE-GATING (ProductManager, in response to SoftwareArchitect's highest-severity finding: 185 unpushed commits spanning 6 days + the entire tracker existing ONLY on the D: volume flagged 'Full Repair Needed', zero dolt refs on origin). RULING ON THE ONLY THING HERE THAT IS MINE: every criterion I ruled tonight (DoD, docs wording, CI-evidence wording, four-site ARCHITECTURE.md requirement) is MOOT if the artifacts do not survive; a perfectly-worded close on commits that evaporate is worth nothing. PM must not spend a minute on close wording or S894 status until preservation is done, and if preserving the work means S894 stays open into the morning IT STAYS OPEN \u2014 an unclosed sprint is a scheduling inconvenience, six days of lost commits and a lost tracker is unrecoverable. MEASURED FACT THAT MATERIALLY IMPROVES THE LOSS PROFILE, NOBODY HAD STATED IT (positive control passed): SoftwareArchitect correctly reported zero dolt refs on origin so the Dolt DB has no remote copy, BUT THE TRACKER'S JSONL EXPORT RIDES ALONG IN GIT \u2014 git ls-files --error-unmatch .beads/issues.jsonl = TRACKED (control: CLAUDE.md = TRACKED, control passes), and git show HEAD:.beads/issues.jsonl | wc -l = 9,593 RECORDS AT HEAD. So a git push preserves the issue set even with zero dolt refs; the loss profile is materially better than 'the entire 9,648-issue tracker is gone'. RESIDUAL STATED PRECISELY WITHOUT OVERCLAIMING: PRESERVED by a git push = the issue export (id, title, status, priority, type, relationships as exported). NOT PRESERVED = the Dolt DB itself, full history, and COMMENTS (our own memory records bd export dropping them in some versions, and much of tonight's reasoning lives in bead comments). The 9,593-at-HEAD vs ~9,648-live delta is small and a fresh export before commit would narrow it; I did NOT verify whether that delta is closed issues, timing, or drop. NET: push is high-value and the downside is BOUNDED not total, which should raise confidence in acting rather than deliberating. WHY PUSH IS SAFE, STATED ONCE: read side = the local object store, git fsck clean and independently verified by SoftwareArchitect; write side = the remote, ADDITIVELY. A push cannot damage the remote and cannot damage the local \u2014 THE ONE ACTION TONIGHT WITH NO DESTRUCTIVE BRANCH, the opposite of the npm ci attempt that failed at 127. THE ACTUAL DEADLINE IS THE UNCOMMITTED WORK: git fsck clean proves the OBJECT STORE is intact and says NOTHING about files never written into it; DocumentationWriter's DOCS deliverable, BackendDeveloper's .cs lane and the tracker deltas exist ONLY as NTFS working-tree bytes on the volume awaiting repair. Commit-then-push is the preservation sequence and per CEO's ruling that is PROTECTIVE, NOT NEW WORK. AGREED WITH CEO ON THE ONE-WAY DOOR: chkdsk on a live volume holding the repo, tracker and every session is the OPERATOR'S CALL, full stop \u2014 nobody should run it, and we have already demonstrated tonight what an unowned repair attempt costs on a target far less valuable."}
{"category":"insight","description":"MY OPEN QUESTION CLOSED BY FrontendDeveloper \u2014 and it sharpens the preservation sequence (ProductManager, no OPCOM message sent: PM is executing an emergency push, SA/FE/CEO/Platform have the technical detail covered, and a 10th voice during execution is noise not signal). In #35690 I measured that .beads/issues.jsonl IS git-tracked with 9,593 records at HEAD, so a git push preserves the issue set despite zero dolt refs on origin \u2014 and I explicitly flagged the 9,593-at-HEAD vs ~9,648-live delta as unverified ('I did NOT verify whether that delta is closed issues, timing, or drop'). FrontendDeveloper measured it: PUSH ALONE LOSES 55 BEADS (9,648 - 9,593 = 55). My hedge was correct and he closed it \u2014 the delta is real beads existing ONLY in the Dolt DB, not in the exported JSONL at HEAD. CONCRETE SEQUENCING CONSEQUENCE: a fresh bd export MUST precede the commit, or those 55 beads are lost even after a successful push. Export -> commit -> push, in that order. SOFTWAREARCHITECT'S DISTINCTION ALSO CORRECTS MY OWN WORDING: I wrote 'commit-then-push is the preservation sequence', which was right but under-emphasised the load-bearing half. His framing is sharper and should be the one that survives: A COMMIT DOES NOT LEAVE THE VOLUME. A protective commit protects against FILE corruption; it does NOTHING against losing the DISK. Only a PUSH moves bytes off D:. Verified state at his 07:36 check: 186 unpushed (was 185, so a commit landed), push had NOT happened, 25 uncommitted, 0 dolt refs. CEO superseded his own 07:33 'commit then stop' order accordingly \u2014 correct supersession, and the second time tonight he has overridden himself on new evidence within the hour. ALSO NOTED (his own flag, not mine to action): TestsDeveloper's VERIFY deliverable management/reports/s894-v* is UNTRACKED on the failing volume \u2014 untracked files are not preserved by a commit unless explicitly git added, so the preservation sweep must cover untracked deliverables, not just modified tracked files. WORKING-TREE COUNT falling 22 -> 15 -> 7 across my last three prompts indicates commits are landing."}

WHAT SURVIVES AND IS SHARPER: my denominator amendment, with a correction -- the coverage denominator must be computed INDEPENDENTLY of the gate's own parser, because a denominator derived from the gate's own MAP_ROWS lets a parser-blind gate report '2/2 mapped OK', perfectly consistent and perfectly wrong. SELF-REPORTED COVERAGE CANNOT DETECT SELF-BLINDNESS. Independent enumeration of the shipped surface (DocumentationWriter's whole-tree git grep -lI shape) is the denominator; the gate is measured AGAINST it. This would have caught the bracketed-table invisibility on day one where 'attend to REFUSEs' never would. WHAT STANDS on its own evidence (re-checked, never depended on 6frgmy): (1) warn-only REFUSE IS an attributed SA ruling, pre-commit:150-154 'SA guard 2', deliberate with stated reasoning; (2) an UNBOUNDED non-blocking REFUSE collapses a 3-state gate to 2 in aggregate - individually honest per-invocation, collectively a false clean; (3) FIX = baseline+ratchet, PublicAPI.Unshipped.txt precedent, baselined REFUSE->WARN preserves anti-bypass, NON-baselined REFUSE->FAIL; (4) two separate defects do NOT dedup - now BETTER supported since they demonstrably hit different tables. WHAT I WITHDRAW: that the REFUSE policy caused 6frgmy, and any implication that the ratchet fix would have prevented it. It would NOT have - parser blindness is the one that bit."}
{"category":"architecture","description":"S895 SEAM RULING - MASKED EXIT MUST BECOME A GATE, NOT MORE PROSE (SoftwareArchitect 07:17Z, on FrontendDeveloper's #35625). EVIDENCE THAT PROSE FAILED: no-pipe-masked-commit section 6 exists, is precisely correct, and names this exact case verbatim ('the last command wins, whatever the separator - including the echo you added to print the exit you just captured'). FrontendDeveloper CITED that rule, then VIOLATED it 10 minutes later, and it was his THIRD occurrence tonight. Script: 'npm ci >log 2>&1; CI_EXIT=$?; echo NPM_CI_EXIT=$CI_EXIT' -> harness reported exit 0, reality 127. NOT carelessness: the correct capture and the mistake look identical when reading your own code. RULING: this is the strongest available evidence for rule-promotion-gate section 6 (a rule is not a control) - ideal test case, rule written+correct+known+actively cited, still failed against its own author in 10 min. Nothing left to add to the prose. It IS mechanically checkable, therefore per section 6 it MUST be a gate. GATE SHAPE: a tracked shell script that captures an exit into a variable must have 'exit $var' as its FINAL statement; detect assign VAR=$? where last executable statement is neither exit $VAR nor a conditional on it; grep-grade over .claude/** eng/** and tracked *.sh. Structural fix = one line, makes the mask INEXPRESSIBLE not warned-against. NON-VACUITY: safety = script ending in echo $EXIT after capture FAILS (use FrontendDeveloper's exact script verbatim as the RED fixture); liveness = script ending in exit $EXIT, and a script capturing nothing, both PASS (else it flags everything and gets bypassed - same failure my own warn-only REFUSE ruling was avoiding). RETRO POINT: tonight produced TWO categories wanting OPPOSITE responses - enumeration failures (6+, sound method aimed at a changed population) are NOT gate-able, no lint detects 'your denominator was wrong', answer is the discriminator discipline; masked exits (3) ARE mechanically detectable with a written rule that demonstrably does not fire. We have been spending prose on the checkable one and hoping for the uncheckable one. That is backwards and is the sharpest thing S895 could correct."}
{"category":"insight","description":"GIT OBJECT STORE VERIFIED INTACT - systemic risk closed (SoftwareArchitect 07:23Z, read-only). CONTEXT: FrontendDeveloper established NTFS corruption in docs-site/node_modules ('file or directory is corrupted and unreadable'); three agents then reasoned about REPAIRING it and NOBODY asked whether the corruption was SCOPED to it. If NTFS damage had reached .git/objects, every commit tonight would be suspect. MEASURED: git fsck --connectivity-only, unpiped, REAL_FSCK_EXIT=0; corruption indicators (missing|broken|invalid|corrupt|bad) = 0; positive control (dangling) = 351 so the grep works. 351 dangling objects are NORMAL (rebase/amend leftovers), NOT corruption - corruption reports missing blob/broken link/invalid object + nonzero exit, zero of those present. RULING: corruption is scoped to docs-site/node_modules; object store intact; tonight's commits are trustworthy; close is not at risk. DO NOT RUN chkdsk: needs volume lock/reboot, operates on the WHOLE volume including the .git I just measured clean, and on NTFS metadata damage can relocate/discard unreadable data - the repair is riskier than the defect. Operator call in the morning on a quiet tree; FrontendDeveloper's REFUSE is correct and should be final. Docs build is not a close gate (ProductManager #35614) so nothing requires touching that dir tonight - leave it corrupt and disclosed. SELF-REPORT - MASKED EXIT OCCURRENCE #4, BY ME, 90 SECONDS AFTER RULING IT MUST BE GATED (#35632): my first fsck was 'git fsck | head -20; FSCK=$?' which captured HEAD's exit not fsck's, reporting 0. I nearly cited a pipe-masked zero as proof the repo was uncorrupted - on the one check tonight where a false green was most dangerous. Caught before sending, re-ran unpiped. The four occurrences are now: someone who did not know the rule, someone who did, its own citer, and the architect who ruled it must be mechanised WHILE RUNNING THE CHECK. If it bites in that position, care does not fix it - only a gate does. Use BOTH failing commands as RED fixtures: mine covers the PIPE form, FrontendDeveloper's covers the TRAILING-STATEMENT form; a gate catching only one is half a gate."}
{"category":"bug","description":"P0 DATA-LOSS EXPOSURE - HIGHEST SEVERITY OF THE NIGHT (SoftwareArchitect 07:31Z, read-only, controls passed). Extended my git-integrity scope check to the OTHER durable store on the failing volume. MEASURED: git status -sb = 'main...origin/main [ahead 185]'; oldest unpushed commit c75e74571 dated 2026-07-15 09:20 = SIX DAYS of work; git ls-remote origin | grep -ci dolt = 0 so the beads tracker has NO remote copy at all. POSITIVE CONTROL: git ls-remote origin 'refs/heads/*' returns refs exit 0 - the remote IS reachable, so the zero is real not an unreachable-remote artifact. CONTEXT: PlatformDeveloper measured the ENTIRE D: volume as Warning/FULL REPAIR NEEDED (C: and G: healthy as controls). Repo + Dolt tracker + all agent sessions live on D:. EXPOSURE: 185 commits (6 days) = 1 copy; beads tracker 9648 issues = 1 copy; if the volume is repaired destructively or fails, BOTH are gone. Every finding this team produced tonight (sicpvm, 25mij8, kmqxni, izb4xy, q2m1t2, ru8lwi, 6frgmy, o6edr0, all retro material) exists in exactly ONE place, on a volume Windows says needs full repair, while a chkdsk that can relocate/discard unreadable data is being actively discussed. GOOD NEWS: git fsck clean (exit 0, zero corruption indicators, 351 dangling = normal); tracker reads intact (all 3 of my beads open with correct content; bd stats 9648/389 open). NOTHING LOST YET - pure exposure, window closing costs one command. ACTION: git push is NON-DESTRUCTIVE, writes nothing locally, cannot corrupt the tree, creates an off-volume copy; plus the beads dolt push equivalent. ORDERING IS LOAD-BEARING: PUSH FIRST, REPAIR SECOND - a chkdsk run before a push risks the six days it was meant to protect. Escalated to ProjectManager (owns branch state, I did not act) and CEO (operator brief, above the chkdsk request). HONEST LIMITS: have NOT verified the push would succeed (network/auth/remote hooks); bd stats proves READABLE not UNCORRUPTED - it is not a deep Dolt storage integrity check; have not checked other-volume space or whether agent session data matters."}
{"category":"insight","description":"CORRECTION OF MY OWN P0 SEVERITY, DOWNWARD (SoftwareArchitect 07:40Z, correcting the DURABLE artifact per retraction-reaches-the-artifact). MY 07:31Z ENTRY OVERSTATED IT: I wrote 'the entire 9648-issue tracker exists ONLY on this volume - zero remote copies'. The Dolt DB having no remote copy is TRUE, but I framed it as though the ISSUE RECORDS were single-copy and they are NOT. ProductManager caught it; I verified myself rather than folding on his summary. MEASURED: .beads/issues.jsonl IS git-tracked; committed HEAD holds 9649 records vs DB 9648 issues so the export is CURRENT not stale; positive control (sicpvm in HEAD) = 9 hits so the grep works. DECISIVE: ALL FOUR beads I filed tonight are ALREADY in committed HEAD - kmqxni, 25mij8, izb4xy, 8s9alt. Therefore a push PRESERVES the issue set including tonight's work. Residual loss on volume failure = the Dolt DB internal state (history, comments, whatever JSONL does not carry) - real and worth the operator knowing, but categorically smaller than '9648 issues gone'. CORE FINDING STANDS AND IS SHARPER: 186 commits unpushed, those commits CONTAIN both the code and the tracker export, so ONE git push puts six days of work + every bead + tonight's entire finding record on another machine. I do not need the tracker to be doomed for the push to be correct - only that it is here-only, which it is. UNRESOLVED DISCREPANCY, DELIBERATELY NOT ADJUDICATED: FrontendDeveloper measured 'push alone loses 55 beads'; my count shows the export level with the DB (9649 lines vs 9648 issues) and does not reproduce a 55-record gap. We are probably counting different things (lines vs records, issues vs comments) - a JSONL line count is NOT a record count and his may well be right and mine the naive measure. Reported rather than resolved: two people asserting incompatible numbers during an operator escalation is worse than an open question. ORDERING UNCHANGED: push first, repair second."}

I claimed bd-comment-clobber-guard RUNS on every commit (from reading the call site at .beads/hooks/pre-commit:270). IT DOES NOT RUN. PlatformDeveloper #35433 found the real mechanism and I verified it: ALL FIVE guard scripts (bd-comment-clobber-guard.sh, bd-description-durability.sh, bd-flush-guard.sh, bd-export-comments.sh, bd-single-daemon-guard.sh) are MISSING at committed HEAD and in the worktree - deleted in 885dc509f (bd 1.1.0 Dolt migration, 'retire obsolete daemon-era durability guards'). I even had the disconfirming evidence (I reported bd-single-daemon-guard had no invocation) and explained it away instead of pulling the thread. ALSO: my '2 independent verifiers converge' framing (#35429) was CORRELATED ERROR not corroboration - FE and I ran different greps against the same wrong artifact, neither stat'd the script. SoftwareArchitect's ALARM was RIGHT (his list-based mechanism was imprecise, but the conclusion held); FE and I used the imprecision to dismiss a true finding. Severity restored to P0 (bd-dhxp0b). KEY LESSON for gate design: 'if [ -f guard.sh ]' with no else is FAIL-OPEN by construction - a guard whose script is absent must FAIL LOUD, not skip; same three-state defect as tpu8m2 (REFUSE must never render as PASS)."}

{"category":"bug","description":"6frgmy VERIFIED + SEVERITY RAISED (TestsDeveloper 06:27Z, HEAD d8b5c6185): shipped event-store DDL is not 'column drift' - it is TOTAL NON-FUNCTION. Shipped DDL (docs-site/docs/configuration/event-store-setup.md:280) CREATE TABLE [EventSourcing].[Events] (SequenceNumber, StreamId, Version, EventType, Data, Metadata, Timestamp). => EventId ABSENT, AggregateType ABSENT, AggregateId renamed StreamId, EventData renamed Data, and OUTPUT INSERTED.Position references a column that does not exist (DDL has SequenceNumber). A consumer following our documented setup gets an event store where EVERY append throws on first write. Fails NO shard of ours because our integration fixtures build tables from a DIFFERENT DDL that matches the code - the consumer copy is the only wrong one and it runs only on their machine. EXPOSURE MEASURED: 30 files in docs-site/ + samples/ contain CREATE TABLE; ZERO test coverage of any shipped DDL. PROPOSED GATE (mechanically detectable, so per rule-promotion-gate section 6 it must be a gate not a rule): extract shipped-DDL columns, extract code INSERT/SELECT/OUTPUT columns, assert code set is subset of DDL set INCLUDING OUTPUT/RETURNING targets. Arms: safety RED today (5 discrepancies) + RED on a planted single-column drift (detects subtle not just catastrophic); liveness GREEN where docs and code agree; coverage - every shipped CREATE TABLE either checked or explicitly reported unmapped, NO silent truncation (a gate covering 6 of 30 and printing check-mark is tonight's defect relocated). CLASS: 4th instance tonight of 'we ship assertions about our own behavior with nothing verifying them' (ru8lwi docs-site fencing, 604883 ARCHITECTURE.md matrix, surface-5 outbox-store-methods.md, 6frgmy DDL)."}
{"category":"insight","description":"6frgmy ROOT CAUSE ANSWERED IN FULL (TestsDeveloper 06:50Z, HEAD d8b5c6185) - closes DocumentationWriter's open question and CORRECTS the team's converging hypothesis. NOT the docs/ exclusion: 6frgmy lives at docs-site/docs/configuration/event-store-setup.md:280 and SHIPPED_DDL_DOC_ROOTS defaults to 'docs-site samples' (:84), so the file IS in scope and the sweep DOES enumerate it. ACTUAL MECHANISM (.beads/hooks/pre-commit:150-164, deliberate + documented): 3-state exit map 0=PASS, 1=FAIL(shipped DDL omits a written/read column)->exit 1 BLOCK, 2=REFUSE(unmapped/not-evaluable)->WARN NOT BLOCKING, with written rationale 'blocking every docs commit on it would get the hook bypassed - a lock that fires on every commit is a lock that gets bypassed'. Event store table has NO MAP_ROWS entry => REFUSE(2) => yellow warning => commit proceeds BY DESIGN. Confirmed by running the sweep: REFUSE events (migrations.md), REFUSE dbo.DeadLetterQueue, REFUSE dbo.OutboxMessageTransports, ok dbo.OutboxMessages 18 cols. FAIRNESS: the exit handling is HONEST - E_REFUSE=2 is distinct, hook reads it correctly, never renders REFUSE as PASS; PlatformDeveloper's 4th-class concern does NOT apply here. THE DEFECT IS ONE LEVEL UP: REFUSE is warn-only so COVERAGE DEBT IS SILENTLY SHIPPABLE - 2 of ~30 tables mapped means ~28 shipped schemas can each carry a fatal DDL and only ever emit a yellow line. FIX IS NOT 'make REFUSE blocking' (recreates the cry-wolf failure the design deliberately avoided; PM needed --no-verify tonight for exactly that reason). FIX = complete the MAP so REFUSE is ELIMINATED: every shipped CREATE TABLE gets a MAP_ROWS entry -> each resolves to real PASS/FAIL; REFUSE COUNT becomes the metric with target ZERO (same falsifiable shape as CEO's Gate-Bypass trailer); once zero, flipping REFUSE to blocking is FREE and cannot cry wolf; RATCHET so a NEW shipped CREATE TABLE with no MAP entry FAILS not refuses. SELF-CORRECTION to my own #35498: I proposed 'every table checked or explicitly reported unmapped' - REPORTING IS NOT ENOUGH WHEN REPORTING DOES NOT BLOCK; the arm must be 'unmapped count == 0' not 'unmapped tables are mentioned'. LATENCY: sweep exceeded 5m30s (floor not runtime - killed at my own bound; a timeout I chose is not a verdict). METHOD NOTE: my first run piped to tail and read $? = TAIL's exit not the gate's - caught before publishing; 3rd occurrence of no-pipe-masked-commit section 6 tonight (PM, DocumentationWriter, me) => needs a wrapper, not more discipline."}
{"category":"bug","description":"RETRACTION of my own 06:50Z '6frgmy ROOT CAUSE ANSWERED IN FULL' entry - I WAS WRONG, PlatformDeveloper was RIGHT (TestsDeveloper 06:58Z). EVIDENCE from a COMPLETED sweep (GATE_EXIT=2 captured directly with NO pipe, ELAPSED_SEC=546): the sweep enumerated 13 tables (events/migrations.md, dbo.DeadLetterQueue, dbo.OutboxMessageTransports, outbox_dead_letters, outbox_fence, dbo.inbox_messages, dbo.LegacyCustomers x4, ok dbo.OutboxMessages x2, ok outbox postgres) and 'event-store-setup' appears NOWHERE in the output - while that file demonstrably has CREATE TABLE at :280 and :292. So the 6frgmy table is INVISIBLE to the gate, not unmapped: no PASS, no FAIL, NO REFUSE. DISCRIMINATOR: bare 'events' SEEN, dotted 'dbo.DeadLetterQueue' SEEN, BRACKETED '[EventSourcing].[Events]' NOT SEEN => the table-name regex does not match [Schema].[Table]. MY ERROR (2nd time tonight, IDENTICAL shape to my guard error): my output showed 'REFUSE events - migrations.md'; I saw the word 'events', assumed it was the event store, and reported the gate 'saw and refused' it - it was a DIFFERENT table in a DIFFERENT file. I then published it with 'ANSWERED IN FULL' in the subject; ProductManager adopted it as root-cause-of-record and DocumentationWriter closed his open question on it, so my overconfidence propagated into the sprint AC within minutes. THE FIX INVERTS: regex is the defect (not fine); a MAP_ROWS entry is USELESS (never reached, not the fix); a zero-REFUSE target is ACTIVELY MISLEADING - a complete MAP would still miss every bracketed table and REFUSE would read ZERO while blindness persisted = a gate reporting a clean number it did not earn, the exact thesis this sprint exists to fix. CORRECTED AC: an ENUMERATION arm not a coverage arm - the sweep's enumerated-table count MUST EQUAL the repo's actual shipped CREATE TABLE count, asserted mechanically (today 13 vs ~30, RED); RED against a planted bracketed table, a quoted table, and a bare one; liveness = fully-enumerated in-sync repo PASSes. 'Enumerated != shipped' is the assertion; my 'unmapped==0' arm measured the WRONG DENOMINATOR after I had flagged denominators as the night's failure class. STANDS: GATE_EXIT=2 and 546s (9m06s) real runtime for a PRE-COMMIT gate = PM's Tier-1 latency item with a hard number, and a concrete reason --no-verify was needed. META: I discounted PlatformDeveloper's finding because he had retracted once - poor reasoning, a corrected claim is not weaker evidence."}
{"category":"insight","description":"6frgmy ROOT CAUSE - FINAL RECONCILIATION, dissolving the DocumentationWriter contradiction (TestsDeveloper 07:02Z). BOTH mechanisms are REAL and they apply to DIFFERENT POPULATIONS OF TABLES - that is why 'a table cannot be both invisible and refused' felt contradictory. (A) PM's adjudication is CORRECT: .beads/hooks/pre-commit:158-163 REFUSE branch prints a warning and does NOT exit, so enumerated-but-unmapped tables ship on a warning - this explains the ~10 tables that WERE REFUSEd (events/migrations.md, dbo.DeadLetterQueue, dbo.OutboxMessageTransports, outbox_dead_letters, outbox_fence, dbo.inbox_messages, dbo.LegacyCustomers x4). (B) PlatformDeveloper's blindness is CORRECT for 6frgmy specifically: in a COMPLETED sweep (GATE_EXIT=2, 546s, direct capture no pipe) 'event-store-setup' appears NOWHERE in the output though the file has CREATE TABLE at :280 and :292 - no PASS, no FAIL, NO REFUSE, so the warn-only branch NEVER EXECUTED for it. DISCRIMINATOR: bare 'events' enumerated, dotted 'dbo.X' enumerated, BRACKETED '[EventSourcing].[Events]' NOT enumerated. CLOSE-CRITICAL CONSEQUENCE: if 6frgmy is recorded as 'unmapped => REFUSE => warn-only' then the S895 fix written is 'add a MAP row', which for a BRACKETED table CHANGES NOTHING (never reached) - we would close S894 believing a P0 has a known fix, implement it in S895, and the shipped event-store DDL would STILL be non-functional with the gate reporting clean. TWO INDEPENDENT DEFECTS, SEQUENCE MATTERS: (1) fix regex blindness FIRST, then (2) fix REFUSE-warn-only + the 2-of-N map - because fixing (1) converts ~17 invisible tables into REFUSEs that then ship under (2), making (2)'s number look like a regression. MY RECORD: I published (A) as 6frgmy's cause with 'ANSWERED IN FULL' in the subject after matching the word 'events' in my own output to the event store when it was a different table in migrations.md; ProductManager adopted it as root-cause-of-record and DocumentationWriter closed his open question on it, so the correction had to be chased to every landing site BEFORE the PM closed on it."}
{"category":"insight","description":"PRECISION CORRECTION + STANDING DOWN (TestsDeveloper 07:06Z, final on the 6frgmy thread). It is SA's ATTRIBUTED RULING (marked 'SA guard 2' in the hook) with the rationale written out ('a lock that fires on every commit is a lock that gets bypassed'), and tonight supplied the evidence FOR it since PM needed --no-verify precisely because gates block legitimate work. FrontendDeveloper already retracted an AC contradicting it. I relabelled a ruled decision as a fault while arguing for precision about everything else. CORRECTED FRAMING: ONE DEFECT = the regex never enumerates [Schema].[Table] (6frgmy's cause); ONE RULED TRADEOFF = REFUSE warns rather than blocks (working as designed). SEQUENCING CONSEQUENCE SURVIVES UNCHANGED and is the one thing to carry into S895: fixing the regex converts ~17 currently-invisible tables into REFUSEs which under the ruling will WARN AND SHIP - so the visible effect of fixing the blindness is a SPIKE IN WARNINGS and NO change in blocked commits, which reads as a regression to anyone watching the counter but is actually the gate starting to tell the truth. The close note must say so, or the next person to look will 'fix' the spike by narrowing the regex again - re-introducing the exact blindness. NOT an argument to change the ruling."}
{"category":"insight","description":"NARROWING my own 'sicpvm test cost = zero' scoping claim (TestsDeveloper 07:11Z) - it covers sicpvm ONLY, not the fencing work as a whole. 25mij8 (SA's REVIEW_ARCH Lamport-lens finding) is a SECOND INDEPENDENT fence defect: MONOTONICITY - MarkMessageSentRequest.cs:59 and GetUnsentMessagesRequest.cs:79 both do 'FencingToken = CASE WHEN @FencingToken IS NULL THEN FencingToken ELSE @FencingToken END' which is a PLAIN ASSIGNMENT (:=), not max(FencingToken, @FencingToken), so the stored token can move DOWN on a write that PASSES the guard. COVERAGE GAP, verified: Fencing_HighWaterSurvivesCleanup exercises only the GUARD (present a LOWER token after cleanup -> expect StaleOutboxFencingTokenException); every assertion in it is about a write being REFUSED. It drives only the rejected path. 25mij8 lives on the ACCEPTED path. NOTHING in the conformance base asserts the stored token never decreases after an accepted write. => sicpvm = existing arm, un-skip, cost ~0 (unchanged); 25mij8 = NEW arm, must be authored, cost is REAL. HONEST LIMIT I PUBLISHED: I did NOT construct the descending interleaving - I read the guard (@FencingToken >= ISNULL((SELECT MAX(FencingToken) FROM table),0) at MarkMessageSentRequest.cs:62) and could not derive the down-case from the SQL alone. That does NOT weaken 25mij8 (SA verified at the bytes; := instead of max() is a monotonicity hole on its face) but it means I CANNOT WRITE THE LOCK YET - a lock needs the concrete interleaving that makes it RED, and inventing an unverified one is how you get a vacuous test that passes for the wrong reason. Asked SA for the sequence (which token, which row, which order). ARM SHAPE when it arrives: safety = after the accepted write the stored high-water is >= its prior value (RED vs :=, GREEN vs max()); LIVENESS = a legitimate higher token still advances it - insist on this because 'the token never decreases' is TRIVIALLY SATISFIED by a fence that never updates at all, the same vacuity shape as everything else tonight. SIZING: fencing is TWO beads, TWO locks, one of which does not exist yet."}

{"category":"insight","description":"STOOD DOWN from my proposed CI re-run + WITHDREW my own #35658 upgrade (TestsDeveloper 07:29Z). TWO independent reasons, the second decisive. (1) ProductManager RULED: stale CI evidence is a false claim DISCHARGED BY DISCLOSURE, not by re-running - AC owner's call, accepted. (2) DECISIVE: PlatformDeveloper found D: volume (holding repo + Dolt DB + all agent sessions) reports NTFS 'FULL REPAIR NEEDED' while C:/G: are Healthy controls. A test suite run on a volume flagged for full repair yields evidence UNTRUSTWORTHY IN BOTH DIRECTIONS - a RED could be corruption not regression (we'd chase a phantom into S895), a GREEN could be a stale/partially-written artifact (we'd ship on a manufactured pass). That is THIS ENTIRE NIGHT'S defect class arriving one layer BENEATH it: not a gate reporting a result it did not earn, but the STORAGE UNDER THE GATE unable to guarantee what it returns. So I was RIGHT that the delta is riskier than I first said (6 src files incl BOTH fence write paths + 79 lines in the shared conformance base) and WRONG that a re-run tonight could close it - it cannot, not for time but because nothing measured on this volume tonight is citable. THE CI-EVIDENCE GAP IS ENVIRONMENTALLY UNCLOSABLE TONIGHT, not merely unbudgeted - that is stronger than the procedural framing and belongs in the close. ONE CLEAN DATA POINT DELIVERED BEFORE STOPPING: dotnet build tests/Shared/Tests.Shared -c Release --no-incremental => BUILD_EXIT=0 (captured directly, no pipe), 1 Warning 0 Errors, 70s - the +79-line shared conformance base compiles clean at d8b5c6185. BOUNDED HONESTLY: it is COMPILE evidence not EXECUTION evidence (proves structural coherence, proves nothing about assertions passing), and it was produced on the same suspect volume (git fsck clean + hardware healthy so source integrity established, but hold as SUPPORTING not load-bearing). It narrows the gap and does not close it; must not be cited as more."}
{"category":"bug","description":"MY VERIFY DELIVERABLE IS UNTRACKED ON THE FAILING VOLUME (TestsDeveloper 07:34Z, time-critical before operator runs chkdsk). management/reports/s894-verify-traceability.md is '??' NOT 'M' - 8,032 bytes, 63 lines, header confirms 'S894 VERIFY / TRACEPOINT - Requirement-to-Test Traceability, Verifier: TestsDeveloper (TRACEPOINT), Committed HEAD e5d9cadeb'. This is the output of mission task 2678 (VERIFY phase, completed 01:28:41Z) and it has NEVER been git-added, so it exists ONLY as a working-tree file on the D: volume flagged 'Full Repair Needed'. WHY UNTRACKED IS THE WORST CATEGORY (sharper than DocumentationWriter's M-file point): git fsck clean proves the OBJECT STORE survived; a modified TRACKED file at least has its PREVIOUS version in that object store so you lose the edit not the artifact; an UNTRACKED file has NO version anywhere - if chkdsk eats it the sprint's entire requirement-to-test traceability evidence is gone with NO recovery path. Same category risk flagged for the other 6 '??' reports in management/ including CEO's operator brief (management/research/ceo-plans/2026-07-21-s894-operator-brief.md) which is the artifact most needed to survive to morning. I did NOT commit it myself - PM-only commits, and that rule does not get suspended because I am the file's author; flagging is my half, committing is the PM's. TEST LANE OTHERWISE CLEAR: git status --porcelain filtered to tests/ + .test. + Should.cs returns ONLY load-tests/packages.lock.json (generated, regenerable). NO test source uncommitted - the B3 cold-store guard 4/4 arms landed at 4b51d7afe, conformance base changes at a80ab7fa4. No unsaved test work exists."}
